[39m[31m<script>[39m[31m\n
(function () {\n
    var overlay = document.getElementById('loginModalOverlay');\n
    if (!overlay) return;\n
\n
    var closeBtn = document.getElementById('loginModalClose');\n
    var toggleBtn = document.getElementById('togglePassword');\n
    var passwordInput = document.getElementById('loginPassword');\n
    var loginForm = overlay.querySelector('.login-modal__form');\n
    var divider = document.getElementById('loginModalDivider');\n
    var guestLink = document.getElementById('loginModalGuest');\n
\n
    function openModal(e, options) {\n
        if (e) e.preventDefault();\n
        overlay.classList.add('is-open');\n
        overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');\n
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';\n
\n
        // If options.hideGuest is true, hide the "Continue as Guest" section\n
        if (options && options.hideGuest && divider && guestLink) {\n
            divider.style.display = 'none';\n
            guestLink.style.display = 'none';\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    function closeModal() {\n
        overlay.classList.remove('is-open');\n
        overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');\n
        document.body.style.overflow = '';\n
        // Discard any pending cart/Checkout action so a stale Buy Now or\n
        // Add-to-Cart does not fire later from a different page.\n
        if (typeof window.clearPendingAction === 'function') {\n
            window.clearPendingAction();\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    // Expose openModal globally so addToCart() can trigger it\n
    // Supports optional options object: { hideGuest: true }\n
    window.openLoginModal = function (e, options) {\n
        openModal(e, options);\n
    };\n
\n
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-login-trigger]').forEach(function (el) {\n
        el.addEventListener('click', function (e) {\n
            // Check if the trigger element has data-hide-guest attribute\n
            var hideGuest = el.getAttribute('data-hide-guest') === 'true';\n
            openModal(e, { hideGuest: hideGuest });\n
        });\n
    });\n
\n
    if (closeBtn) closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeModal);\n
\n
    overlay.addEventListener('click', function (e) {\n
        if (e.target === overlay) closeModal();\n
    });\n
\n
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {\n
        if (e.key === 'Escape' && overlay.classList.contains('is-open')) closeModal();\n
    });\n
\n
    if (toggleBtn && passwordInput) {\n
        toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n
            var isPassword = passwordInput.type === 'password';\n
            passwordInput.type = isPassword ? 'text' : 'password';\n
            toggleBtn.innerHTML = '[39m[31m<i data-lucide="' + (isPassword ? 'eye-off' : 'eye') + '" width="18" height="18">[39m[31m</i>[39m[31m';\n
            if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();\n
        });\n
    }\n
\n
    // Success callback — called after successful login/register.\n
    // The login form submits via classic POST (page reload), so this fires\n
    // on the next page load if a pending cart action exists.\n
    window.addEventListener('load', function () {\n
        if (typeof retryPendingCartAction === 'function') {\n
            retryPendingCartAction();\n
        }\n
    });\n
})();\n
[39m[31m</script>[39m[31m\n

[39m[31m<link rel="modulepreload" as="script" href="https://zefanya.shop/build/assets/address-lookup-DmSFI5n-.js" />[39m[31m<script type="module" src="https://zefanya.shop/build/assets/address-lookup-DmSFI5n-.js">[39m[31m</script>[39m[31m<script>[39m[31m\n
    // Account-page bootstrapping: scope the shared lookup to THIS form (the\n
    // layout also embeds the registration modal whose duplicate IDs would\n
    // otherwise win a document-wide getElementById), then restore the saved\n
    // record's mode and preselect its PSGC codes.\n
    (function boot() {\n
        var root = document.querySelector('form[action*="account/address"]');\n
        if (!root || !window.zfAddressLookup) {\n
            // Module is a deferred Vite entry — retry until it lands.\n
            return setTimeout(boot, 25);\n
        }\n
\n
        var api = window.zfAddressLookup.init(root);\n
        if (!api) return;\n
\n
        var saved = {\n
            province: root.querySelector('#province').dataset.savedCode || '',\n
            municipality: root.querySelector('#municipality').dataset.savedCode || '',\n
            barangay: root.querySelector('#barangay').dataset.savedCode || '',\n
        };\n
\n
        if (root.querySelector('#addressMode').value === 'manual') {\n
            // Saved record used manual entry — start there. setManualMode also\n
            // attaches the mirror listeners that keep the hidden *_name fields\n
            // in sync with what the user types.\n
            api.setManualMode(true);\n
        } else if (saved.province) {\n
            api.preselect(saved);\n
        } else {\n
            api.ensureLoaded();\n
        }\n
    })();\n

[39m[31m<script>[39m[31m\n
(function () {\n
    var overlay = document.getElementById('loginModalOverlay');\n
    if (!overlay) return;\n
\n
    var closeBtn = document.getElementById('loginModalClose');\n
    var toggleBtn = document.getElementById('togglePassword');\n
    var passwordInput = document.getElementById('loginPassword');\n
    var loginForm = overlay.querySelector('.login-modal__form');\n
    var divider = document.getElementById('loginModalDivider');\n
    var guestLink = document.getElementById('loginModalGuest');\n
\n
    function openModal(e, options) {\n
        if (e) e.preventDefault();\n
        overlay.classList.add('is-open');\n
        overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');\n
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';\n
\n
        // If options.hideGuest is true, hide the "Continue as Guest" section\n
        if (options && options.hideGuest && divider && guestLink) {\n
            divider.style.display = 'none';\n
            guestLink.style.display = 'none';\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    function closeModal() {\n
        overlay.classList.remove('is-open');\n
        overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');\n
        document.body.style.overflow = '';\n
        // Discard any pending cart/Checkout action so a stale Buy Now or\n
        // Add-to-Cart does not fire later from a different page.\n
        if (typeof window.clearPendingAction === 'function') {\n
            window.clearPendingAction();\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    // Expose openModal globally so addToCart() can trigger it\n
    // Supports optional options object: { hideGuest: true }\n
    window.openLoginModal = function (e, options) {\n
        openModal(e, options);\n
    };\n
\n
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-login-trigger]').forEach(function (el) {\n
        el.addEventListener('click', function (e) {\n
            // Check if the trigger element has data-hide-guest attribute\n
            var hideGuest = el.getAttribute('data-hide-guest') === 'true';\n
            openModal(e, { hideGuest: hideGuest });\n
        });\n
    });\n
\n
    if (closeBtn) closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeModal);\n
\n
    overlay.addEventListener('click', function (e) {\n
        if (e.target === overlay) closeModal();\n
    });\n
\n
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {\n
        if (e.key === 'Escape' && overlay.classList.contains('is-open')) closeModal();\n
    });\n
\n
    if (toggleBtn && passwordInput) {\n
        toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n
            var isPassword = passwordInput.type === 'password';\n
            passwordInput.type = isPassword ? 'text' : 'password';\n
            toggleBtn.innerHTML = '[39m[31m<i data-lucide="' + (isPassword ? 'eye-off' : 'eye') + '" width="18" height="18">[39m[31m</i>[39m[31m';\n
            if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();\n
        });\n
    }\n
\n
    // Success callback — called after successful login/register.\n
    // The login form submits via classic POST (page reload), so this fires\n
    // on the next page load if a pending cart action exists.\n
    window.addEventListener('load', function () {\n
        if (typeof retryPendingCartAction === 'function') {\n
            retryPendingCartAction();\n
        }\n
    });\n
})();\n
[39m[31m</script>[39m[31m\n

[39m[31m<script>[39m[31m\n
(function () {\n
    var overlay = document.getElementById('loginModalOverlay');\n
    if (!overlay) return;\n
\n
    var closeBtn = document.getElementById('loginModalClose');\n
    var toggleBtn = document.getElementById('togglePassword');\n
    var passwordInput = document.getElementById('loginPassword');\n
    var loginForm = overlay.querySelector('.login-modal__form');\n
    var divider = document.getElementById('loginModalDivider');\n
    var guestLink = document.getElementById('loginModalGuest');\n
\n
    function openModal(e, options) {\n
        if (e) e.preventDefault();\n
        overlay.classList.add('is-open');\n
        overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');\n
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';\n
\n
        // If options.hideGuest is true, hide the "Continue as Guest" section\n
        if (options && options.hideGuest && divider && guestLink) {\n
            divider.style.display = 'none';\n
            guestLink.style.display = 'none';\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    function closeModal() {\n
        overlay.classList.remove('is-open');\n
        overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');\n
        document.body.style.overflow = '';\n
        // Discard any pending cart/Checkout action so a stale Buy Now or\n
        // Add-to-Cart does not fire later from a different page.\n
        if (typeof window.clearPendingAction === 'function') {\n
            window.clearPendingAction();\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    // Expose openModal globally so addToCart() can trigger it\n
    // Supports optional options object: { hideGuest: true }\n
    window.openLoginModal = function (e, options) {\n
        openModal(e, options);\n
    };\n
\n
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-login-trigger]').forEach(function (el) {\n
        el.addEventListener('click', function (e) {\n
            // Check if the trigger element has data-hide-guest attribute\n
            var hideGuest = el.getAttribute('data-hide-guest') === 'true';\n
            openModal(e, { hideGuest: hideGuest });\n
        });\n
    });\n
\n
    if (closeBtn) closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeModal);\n
\n
    overlay.addEventListener('click', function (e) {\n
        if (e.target === overlay) closeModal();\n
    });\n
\n
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {\n
        if (e.key === 'Escape' && overlay.classList.contains('is-open')) closeModal();\n
    });\n
\n
    if (toggleBtn && passwordInput) {\n
        toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n
            var isPassword = passwordInput.type === 'password';\n
            passwordInput.type = isPassword ? 'text' : 'password';\n
            toggleBtn.innerHTML = '[39m[31m<i data-lucide="' + (isPassword ? 'eye-off' : 'eye') + '" width="18" height="18">[39m[31m</i>[39m[31m';\n
            if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();\n
        });\n
    }\n
\n
    // Success callback — called after successful login/register.\n
    // The login form submits via classic POST (page reload), so this fires\n
    // on the next page load if a pending cart action exists.\n
    window.addEventListener('load', function () {\n
        if (typeof retryPendingCartAction === 'function') {\n
            retryPendingCartAction();\n
        }\n
    });\n
})();\n
[39m[31m</script>[39m[31m\n

[39m[31m<script>[39m[31m\n
(function () {\n
    var overlay = document.getElementById('loginModalOverlay');\n
    if (!overlay) return;\n
\n
    var closeBtn = document.getElementById('loginModalClose');\n
    var toggleBtn = document.getElementById('togglePassword');\n
    var passwordInput = document.getElementById('loginPassword');\n
    var loginForm = overlay.querySelector('.login-modal__form');\n
    var divider = document.getElementById('loginModalDivider');\n
    var guestLink = document.getElementById('loginModalGuest');\n
\n
    function openModal(e, options) {\n
        if (e) e.preventDefault();\n
        overlay.classList.add('is-open');\n
        overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'false');\n
        document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';\n
\n
        // If options.hideGuest is true, hide the "Continue as Guest" section\n
        if (options && options.hideGuest && divider && guestLink) {\n
            divider.style.display = 'none';\n
            guestLink.style.display = 'none';\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    function closeModal() {\n
        overlay.classList.remove('is-open');\n
        overlay.setAttribute('aria-hidden', 'true');\n
        document.body.style.overflow = '';\n
        // Discard any pending cart/Checkout action so a stale Buy Now or\n
        // Add-to-Cart does not fire later from a different page.\n
        if (typeof window.clearPendingAction === 'function') {\n
            window.clearPendingAction();\n
        }\n
    }\n
\n
    // Expose openModal globally so addToCart() can trigger it\n
    // Supports optional options object: { hideGuest: true }\n
    window.openLoginModal = function (e, options) {\n
        openModal(e, options);\n
    };\n
\n
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-login-trigger]').forEach(function (el) {\n
        el.addEventListener('click', function (e) {\n
            // Check if the trigger element has data-hide-guest attribute\n
            var hideGuest = el.getAttribute('data-hide-guest') === 'true';\n
            openModal(e, { hideGuest: hideGuest });\n
        });\n
    });\n
\n
    if (closeBtn) closeBtn.addEventListener('click', closeModal);\n
\n
    overlay.addEventListener('click', function (e) {\n
        if (e.target === overlay) closeModal();\n
    });\n
\n
    document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {\n
        if (e.key === 'Escape' && overlay.classList.contains('is-open')) closeModal();\n
    });\n
\n
    if (toggleBtn && passwordInput) {\n
        toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', function () {\n
            var isPassword = passwordInput.type === 'password';\n
            passwordInput.type = isPassword ? 'text' : 'password';\n
            toggleBtn.innerHTML = '[39m[31m<i data-lucide="' + (isPassword ? 'eye-off' : 'eye') + '" width="18" height="18">[39m[31m</i>[39m[31m';\n
            if (window.lucide) lucide.createIcons();\n
        });\n
    }\n
\n
    // Success callback — called after successful login/register.\n
    // The login form submits via classic POST (page reload), so this fires\n
    // on the next page load if a pending cart action exists.\n
    window.addEventListener('load', function () {\n
        if (typeof retryPendingCartAction === 'function') {\n
            retryPendingCartAction();\n
        }\n
    });\n
})();\n
